# Reconciled Investigative Public-Record API Catalogue — Volume 1 of 5

Systems SYS-0001 through SYS-0267. This is a companion source to `investigative_api_catalogue_00_guide.txt`. Each system record remains intact with its access points, coverage mappings, ranking, and verification material. Stable identifiers should be used when joining records across volumes.

## SYS-0001 — GLEIF LEI data

- **Publisher:** Global Legal Entity Identifier Foundation
- **Official domain:** gleif.org
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Global / multi-jurisdiction
- **Coarse record family:** Corporate, ownership & financial records
- **Official or non-official status:** Mixed / not established
- **Geographic coverage:** Global
- **Access modes:** API / web service; Bulk / download; API / web service; API / web service; Search / web portal
- **Representative URL:** https://www.gleif.org/en/lei-data/gleif-api
- **Source-system confidence:** Medium

### Editorial ranking
- **Rank:** 524
- **Final active:** YES
- **Verification status:** BASELINE_CARRIED_FORWARD
- **Verification date:** 2026-08-05
- **Total score:** 75
- **Component scores:** HNWI relevance: 5; Source authority: 3; Person/entity linkage: 5; Legal reuse confidence: 1

### Access points

#### AP-0001 — GLEIF LEI data
- **Layer:** Established
- **Original ID:** SRC-001
- **Normalized record family:** Corporate, ownership & financial records
- **Normalized jurisdiction:** Global / multi-jurisdiction
- **Access-point type:** API / web service; Bulk / download
- **Original access method:** REST API; daily concatenated files; delta files
- **Representative access URL:** https://www.gleif.org/en/lei-data/gleif-api
- **Documentation URL:** https://www.gleif.org/en/lei-data/gleif-api
- **Investigative relevance or methodology:** Authoritative Legal Entity Identifier records, registered names, jurisdictions, addresses, and parent relationships; an unusually strong cross-border entity spine.
- **Cost or eligibility:** €0
- **Refresh cadence:** Daily
- **Useful join fields:** LEI; legal name; registration authority ID; parent LEI
- **Main weakness:** Only entities that have obtained an LEI; parent reporting can contain exceptions.
- **Legal tier:** A
- **Overall score out of 5:** 4.9

- **Original access method:** REST API; filing documents; streaming interfaces

- **Documentation URL:** https://developer-specs.company-information.service.gov.uk/companies-house-public-data-api/reference
- **Investigative relevance or methodology:** Companies, filings, officers, charges, insolvency and people with significant control.
- **Cost or eligibility:** €0; registration and API key
- **Refresh cadence:** Near-real-time
- **Useful join fields:** Company number; officer ID; person-with-significant-control ID; address
- **Main weakness:** Filed data is not verified by Companies House; historical document retrieval is uneven.
- **Legal tier:** A
- **Overall score out of 5:** 4.9

## SYS-0055 — SAM.gov Contract Data API

### Public-interest journalism update — 2026-09-23

This dated review supersedes conflicting access and scope claims in the legacy entry below. Source: the user's two batches of GSA/SAM documentation (32 PDFs, representing 30 distinct document titles after collapsing two duplicate exports). The supplied PDFs were reviewed for purpose, access, response structure and limitations; this is not a line-by-line validation of every schema field. The Contract Awards, Acquisition Gateway and IT Collect pages were also checked online. No authenticated SAM request was made: logging into SAM in a browser does not provide this collection process with an API key.

Existing SYS/AP identifiers and historical ranking scores are retained. The review items RAB-GSA-20260923-01 through -30 below are local audit identifiers, NOT newly allocated global SYS/AP identifiers. Non-SAM sources are recorded as related source candidates pending reconciliation across Rabbit's other volumes; they are not counted as new unique systems. This update modifies the supplied September copy Rabbit2(1).txt, Volume 1; it does not silently alter older duplicate copies or other volumes.

**Pilot priority:** collect public opportunity notices and available attachments for evidence of intended work; public award/modification records for commitments and changes; subcontract records for reported downstream beneficiaries. Use USAspending SYS-0054 for inexpensive broad collection and financial context. These sources overlap and are not independent confirmation merely because they appear on separate government sites.

**Public-interest questions:** What new work is proposed in a particular country? Which studies or threat assessments are being commissioned? Has a programme quietly expanded or been curtailed? Who benefits below the prime contractor? What changed in competition, public cost or eligibility? A small study can matter without a large dollar amount. A country mention alone does not establish a new base; a solicitation does not establish an award, and an award does not establish completed activity.

**Evidence discipline:** preserve source bytes and source address, notice/award/transaction identifiers, modification/version, signed/action date, posted date where supplied, last-modified date, first observed date and retrieval date. Keep obligations, ceilings and payments distinct. Match the specific development to news, not just the programme name. Distinguish military work from Army Corps civilian projects. Record failed downloads and access-denied attachments as missing evidence. An API description is documentation-reviewed until a successful authorised request verifies it.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-01 — Contract Awards: expand AP-0056
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ; high priority; documentation verified, not live-tested here.
- **Publisher / coverage:** U.S. General Services Administration; federal award and indefinite-delivery vehicle data, including orders, modifications and Other Transaction records as documented. Structured data, not a library of signed contract PDFs.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/contract-awards/v1/search
- **Documentation / evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/contract-awards/ ; attached Contract Awards PDF pp. 2–7; live page checked 2026-09-23.
- **Access and cost:** free public/revealed data with SAM personal API key. Default ordinary non-federal account with no SAM role: 10 requests/day; documented role-associated personal account: 1,000/day. Higher limits require actual eligibility, not an invented affiliation.
- **Scale:** default 10, maximum 100 records/page; search caps at first 400,000 records. Asynchronous format=csv or format=json extracts cap at 1,000,000. Narrow date/agency partitions and verify completeness; download/status requests can consume additional quota. Do not assume an extract is one unlimited call.
- **Timing and restricted fields:** contracts both funded and awarded by Defense enter the revealed tier when signed at least 90 days earlier; civilian-funded or civilian-awarded records are treated differently. Immediate-parent and domestic-parent names and entity identifiers are excluded from the revealed response. Do not promise an ownership graph from public fields.
- **Useful joins:** Procurement Instrument Identifier (PIID), agency, referenced vehicle identifier, modification/transaction identifiers, solicitation ID, awardee Unique Entity ID, Commercial and Government Entity code, places and dates. PIID alone can be ambiguous.
- **Methods:** distinguish transaction dollarsObligated from totalDollarsObligated; inspect modifications and descriptions; compare competition and number of offers where supplied. piidAggregation summarises award families and related orders; do not add those totals to their components. deletedStatus retrieves deleted contracts within the documented six-month window; deletion can be correction, not concealment.
- **Reporting value:** growth beyond launch coverage, changes in beneficiary, concentrated suppliers, a change in location or purpose. Source disclosure delay must be included in historical news comparisons.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-02 — Get Opportunities Public API
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ; highest priority for purpose/geography discovery.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/opportunities/v2/search
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/get-opportunities-public-api/ ; attached PDF pp. 2–10.
- **Contents:** pre-solicitations, sources-sought notices, solicitations, special notices, award notices and justifications. Description is a retrieval link; resourceLinks points to available attachments, not guaranteed extracted text.
- **Access / scale:** SAM personal key; documentation states role-dependent daily limits but does not provide a numeric table here. Do not automatically transfer another endpoint's quota. Posted date range required, maximum one year per query; maximum 1,000 results/page. Use current organizationCode/organizationName rather than deprecated deptname/subtier filters.
- **History:** search provides latest active version; documentation directs historical-version users to Data Services. Active notices updated daily, archived weekly. Archive our own observations; do not assume a current attachment existed on the original posting date.
- **Joins / reporting:** notice ID, solicitation number, agency, dates, named places and award details when present. Study terms of reference, planned base support or closures, unusual research questions and sole-source explanations. No minimum dollar threshold for this lane. Download public attachments separately and respect their individual access restrictions; this public search is not the restricted Opportunity Management interface.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-03 — Acquisition Subaward Reporting Public API
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ; high priority after prime-award matching.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/prod/contract/v1/subcontracts/search
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/acquisition-subaward-reporting-api/ ; attached PDF pp. 2–6.
- **Contents / joins:** published and deleted subcontract reports; link through prime contract key, agency, prime identifier and referenced vehicle identifier, plus reported recipient identifiers and dates.
- **Access / scale:** personal key; 10/day for non-federal user without entity role; 1,000/day with role; default 100, maximum 1,000 rows/page. Query Published and Deleted separately; missing status defaults to Published.
- **Reporting value / limits:** identify reported beneficiaries and compare prime/subcontract relationships across programmes. Reporting omissions, thresholds, revisions and lag mean this is not a complete supply-chain map. Payments to subcontractors must not be added to prime totals as extra government spending. The PDF's overview mistakenly shows an assistance endpoint example; use its dedicated production contract endpoint above, then validate live.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-04 — Assistance Subaward Reporting Public API
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ; medium for broader grant/aid accountability, secondary for procurement pilot.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/prod/assistance/v1/subawards/search
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/assistance-subaward-reporting-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–6.
- **Contents / joins:** published/deleted assistance subaward reports; Federal Award Identification Number, agency code, prime award key and unique award key.
- **Access / scale:** personal key; ordinary no-role non-federal account 10/day; default 100, maximum 1,000 rows/page. Separate deletion queries and retain revisions.
- **Reporting value / limits:** trace reported grant intermediaries and downstream recipients, including research/aid where present. Do not combine assistance and procurement into a single transaction category or assume all subawards are reported.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-05 — Entity Management: public tier only
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ for public fields; medium, targeted enrichment.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/entity-information/v4/entities
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/entity-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–6.
- **Contents:** public names, entity identifiers, registration status, addresses, business types and industry/product codes. Public responsibility/integrity records may help where documented; inspect their coverage rather than infer it from company status.
- **Access / scale:** personal key for public tier; paginated search default 10 records and first 10,000 cap; extract route offers CSV/JSON up to first 1,000,000. See endpoint-specific role limits before collection.
- **Reporting value / limits:** resolve recipients, track name/address/registration changes, support exclusions matching. Not an unrestricted beneficial-ownership register. Public-display opt-outs and controlled hierarchy/contact fields create gaps; sensitive financial and tax identifiers are not public data.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-06 — Exclusions API
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ; high-value targeted accountability enrichment.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/entity-information/v4/exclusions
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/exclusions-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–4.
- **Contents:** public exclusions, including documented supply-chain security orders. Search response is paginated, default 10 records; first 10,000 cap. Extract route up to 1,000,000 records. Ordinary non-federal no-role account: 10 requests/day.
- **Joins / method:** resolve entity identifiers where present plus names, addresses, excluding agency, classification and effective/termination dates. Match eligibility at the relevant award date and read the exclusion's scope; similar names or a current exclusion do not establish an unlawful earlier award.
- **Limits:** this is not the Treasury sanctions list and the two must not be treated as interchangeable. Retain false-match checks from the earlier OFAC experiment.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-07 — Entity / Exclusions Extracts Download
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ for public extracts; medium/high for efficient local joins.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/data-services/v1/extracts
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/sam-entity-extracts-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–6.
- **Contents / cadence:** monthly public entity files cover active entities and those expired within six months; daily public exclusions files cover active exclusions; separate daily supply-chain order extracts are documented. Daily entity deltas described in this PDF are controlled/sensitive, not a promised public feed.
- **Method / limits:** cache public snapshots for repeated local matching. A current active-only exclusions file cannot establish historical eligibility; keep dated snapshots or seek suitable historical records. Inspect file-date conventions and coverage before treating absence as a change.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-08 — Product and Service Code public lookup
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ; supporting reference data.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/prod/locationservices/v1/api/publicpscdetails
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/PSC-Public-API/ ; PDF pp. 2–6.
- **Contents / access:** codes, names, inclusions/exclusions, hierarchy and validity dates. Non-federal documented limit 10/day; cache the table.
- **Use:** decode research and development, studies, construction and maintenance categories before model classification. A code is a routing signal, not proof of subject matter or significance. Preserve historic code validity.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-09 — Federal Hierarchy Public API
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ; supporting reference data.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/prod/federalorganizations/v1/ (documented orgs and org/hierarchy routes).
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/fh-public-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–4.
- **Contents / access:** active/inactive department and sub-tier organisations; default 10, maximum 100/page; non-federal limit 10/day.
- **Use / limits:** normalise awarding/funding agencies and distinguish different organisations with similar names. Public coverage is the first two levels, not a complete public roster of military offices or commands.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-10 — Assistance Listings Public API
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PERSONAL-KEY READ; medium for grant-policy reporting.
- **Endpoint:** https://api.sam.gov/assistance-listings/v1/search
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/assistance-listings-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–4.
- **Contents / access:** active/inactive assistance programme descriptions, eligibility, objectives and financial context; personal key, ordinary no-role account 10/day, maximum 1,000/page.
- **Use / limits:** find new or changed programme priorities and connect programme numbers to awards. Programme listings are not award transactions and cannot establish that a named recipient received funds.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-11 — SAM Data Services portal
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC PORTAL / DISCOVERY; supporting, not a separate verified API.
- **Evidence:** attached SAM.gov | Data Services.pdf, all 3 pages; https://sam.gov/data-services/Data%20Dictionary/Contract%20Awards?privacy=Public
- **What this upload proves:** the logged-in interface lists contract-award dictionaries and specifications, including Atom and multiple FPDS versions. It does not itself establish that any particular bulk data export is available or complete.
- **Use:** locate matching current schema files and investigate historical opportunity extracts. Treat that as a separate collection test, not a capability already demonstrated by this screenshot export.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-12 — FPDS Atom Feed Specifications V1.5.3
- **Status / priority:** LEGACY SCHEMA REFERENCE; useful mapping, live feed availability untested.
- **Evidence:** attached FPDS_Atom_Feed_Specifications_V1.5.3.pdf; https://www.fpds.gov/wiki/index.php/Atom_Feed_Specifications_V_1.5.3
- **Useful fields:** agency/contract identifier, modification and transaction numbers, signed/effective dates, reason for modification, description of requirement, extent competed and number of offers. The document includes award, vehicle and Other Transaction examples.
- **Use / limits:** map legacy data and design comparisons of changed records. Examples are a subset of the full schema. The document's real-time language is not proof of current public Defense disclosure timing; do not use it to override the 90-day rule in current Contract Awards documentation.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-13 — FPDS Data Dictionary V1.2
- **Status / priority:** HISTORICAL REFERENCE, not a data feed or current schema authority.
- **Evidence:** attached FPDS_Data_Dictionary_V1.2.pdf; cover dated September 23, 2015, with much older revision history.
- **Use:** understand distinctions among dates, obligations, potential values, competition and modification reasons in legacy records. Field names/definitions must be checked against the actual version returned now. The filename version is not evidence of freshness.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-14 — FPDS NASA Specific Data Dictionary
- **Status / priority:** HISTORICAL AGENCY-SPECIFIC REFERENCE; low for Pentagon pilot.
- **Evidence:** attached FPDS_NASA_Specific_Data_Dictionary.pdf; cover May 26, 2006, revision history includes December 2012.
- **Use / limits:** historical NASA proposal-to-award linkage and procurement coding. NASA-specific proposal fields must not be assumed present in Defense data. This document is not an additional API.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-15 — Subcontracting Plan Reporting Outbound
- **Status:** RESTRICTED; exclude from ordinary public-key collection.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.sam.gov/spr/v1/search ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/spr-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–3.
- **Potential value:** compare reported subcontracting goals and actuals, if records become available through authorised disclosure.
- **Access:** federal system account with Read FOUO permission for Subcontracting Plan Reports, approved connection/IP and authentication. Its read/outbound label does not make it a public source. Use public acquisition subaward records for the pilot instead; those are a different dataset.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-16 — Opportunity Management API
- **Status:** AUTHORISED GOVERNMENT MANAGEMENT; not our public retrieval route.
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/opportunities-api/ ; PDF pp. 3–4.
- **Function / access:** create, revise, publish and manage notices; includes read/history/attachment operations but requires the documented government system account and permissions. Some operations are marked unavailable and release notes are inconsistent.
- **Decision:** do not treat listed history or download-all operations as capabilities of an ordinary public key. Use Get Opportunities Public API and only attachments available under the user's actual access.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-17 — Subaward Reporting Bulk Upload
- **Status:** REPORTER WRITE WORKFLOW; exclude from news collection.
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/subawards-bulkupload-api/ ; PDF pp. 3–7.
- **Function / access:** submission, updates and deletion of reports by authorised reporting entities; system account, reporting permissions, registered IP and testing requirements. Listed retrieval operations do not imply unrestricted public reporting data. Use public acquisition/assistance subaward read APIs instead.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-18 — Federal Hierarchy For Official Use Only
- **Status:** RESTRICTED; exclude from public collection.
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/fh-fouo-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–4.
- **Access / limits:** government users, deeper office/command hierarchy; restrictions on dissemination. Documentation explicitly prohibits using it to build a public hierarchy. Use the public department/sub-tier endpoint, and office fields already lawfully published with award records.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-19 — Public Location Services
- **Status:** FEDERAL SYSTEM KEY / PURPOSE-LIMITED; exclude despite public label.
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/location-public-api/ ; PDF p. 2.
- **Access / limits:** documentation requires a valid federal government system account key and limits use to validating locations submitted to SAM. SAM is not an authoritative gazetteer.
- **Decision:** use another public geographic reference for place normalisation. This is not a military-facilities dataset.

### Related public-interest source review (cross-volume reconciliation pending)

These items are recorded here because they were supplied with the SAM documentation. They are not silently assigned to the SAM source system or counted as new globally unique sources.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-20 — Regulations.gov read APIs
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC API.KEY READ; high for broader public-interest journalism, complementary to procurement.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.regulations.gov/v4/ ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/regulationsgov/ ; PDF pp. 5–8 and retrieval examples/FAQ.
- **Access:** API.data.gov key for read requests; separate activation requirements apply to posting comments, which is outside this collection workflow.
- **Contents / joins:** documents, comments, dockets and available attachments; document ID, docket ID, agency and dates. Request attachments explicitly on detail calls; they are not included by default. Partition large comment retrievals to respect the documented pagination ceiling.
- **Reporting questions:** which companies seek exemptions or changes, what evidence underpins a rule, and what consequences are buried in supporting reports? Link claims to original submissions. Comment counts do not measure public opinion; repeated campaigns and agency-controlled field visibility complicate interpretation. A missing comment/attachment can reflect publication processing or restrictions.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-21 — CALC+ labor ceiling rates
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC READ; medium for pricing context.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.gsa.gov/acquisition/calc/v3/api/ceilingrates/ ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/dx-calc-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–4.
- **Access / freshness:** documentation says no user credentials/authentication; daily overnight refresh. Legacy v2 rates API retired February 2025; do not build against it.
- **Joins / use:** vendor, contract number, labor category, worksite, education, experience, clearance and current price. Compare like-for-like published labor ceilings and changes in schedules.
- **Critical limit:** ceiling rates are not wages, actual invoice rates or proof of overcharging. Match job requirements and contract terms before drawing comparisons.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-22 — Data.gov catalogue API
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC KEY READ; high for source discovery, not direct event detection.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.gsa.gov/technology/datagov/v3/ ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/datadotgov/ ; PDF pp. 1–3.
- **Access / contents:** API.data.gov key; CKAN catalogue metadata, dataset descriptions and resource links, NOT the underlying data.
- **Use / limits:** find additional agency datasets and observe catalogue changes; fetch actual records from linked publishers. A catalogue deletion alone does not prove that underlying data vanished or that a policy changed.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-23 — Site Scanning
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC KEY READ / BULK; medium for digital transparency reporting.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.gsa.gov/technology/site-scanning/v1/websites ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/site-scanning-api/ ; PDF pp. 2–4 and download section.
- **Access / contents:** API.data.gov key; direct CSV/JSON download alternative; daily scans of federal websites, liveness/redirect and agency ownership fields among other scan results.
- **Use / limits:** seed agency website monitoring and identify persistent losses of public access. Save repeated observations: one failed scan or redirect is not proof of censorship, disappearance of a programme, or closure of an agency.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-24 — analytics.usa.gov
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC KEY READ; medium for public-service demand/access questions; low for procurement.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.gsa.gov/analytics/dap/v2/ ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/dap/ ; PDF pp. 3–6.
- **Access / contents:** API.data.gov key; aggregate reports for participating government domains/agencies. Version 2 uses Google Analytics 4 data beginning August 2023; older version is a different measurement series.
- **Limits:** only qualifying sites with minimum 50 visits in the reporting period; sampling and participation gaps. Not individual visitor tracking, a census of public interest, or a measure of newspaper coverage. Avoid treating the analytics-version break as a real change in demand.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-25 — API.data.gov Metrics
- **Status / priority:** PUBLIC KEY READ; low/adjacent digital-government accountability.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.gsa.gov/operations/api-data-gov/v0/analytics/summary.json ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/apidatagov-metrics/ ; PDF pp. 1–3.
- **Contents / use:** high-level shared-service traffic and adoption totals. API use is not a count of unique people or evidence of military activity. Several field descriptions appear copied incorrectly in the document; verify returned schema before quantitative use.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-26 — API.data.gov Admin
- **Status:** ADMINISTRATIVE / RESTRICTED; exclude.
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/apidatagov/ ; PDF pp. 1–2.
- **Access / function:** ordinary API key AND admin authentication token; administrative operations and agency analytics. Public documentation is not public data access. Metrics endpoint above is the public alternative for aggregate service statistics.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-27 — SearchGov results
- **Status:** AFFILIATE-SCOPED SEARCH INTEGRATION; not a general free news/web search service.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.gsa.gov/technology/searchgov/v2/results/i14y ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/searchgov-results/ ; PDF pp. 2–5.
- **Access / contents:** affiliate site handle and corresponding access key; searches configured/indexed domain scope and returns titles, addresses, snippets and dates. Documented default 1,000 requests/hour; maximum 50 results per request and offset 999.
- **Decision:** consider only with an authorised affiliate arrangement; it does not supply a general replacement for GDELT or the pilot's independent coverage search. Do not reuse credentials exposed by unrelated sites.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-28 — SearchGov clicks
- **Status:** TELEMETRY SUBMISSION; exclude.
- **Endpoint / evidence:** https://api.gsa.gov/technology/searchgov/v2/clicks/ ; https://open.gsa.gov/api/searchgov-clicks/ ; PDF pp. 1–3.
- **Function:** POST click events for an affiliate search experience. It does not let journalists download users' search/click histories. Do not send synthetic events while testing collection.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-29 — Acquisition Gateway Public Listings
- **Status:** DOCUMENTATION CONFLICT / PLACEHOLDER; not collection-ready.
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/ag-api/ ; PDF pp. 1–3; live page checked 2026-09-23.
- **Reason:** page says no APIs are available and documents TBD responses, while also claiming v4.0 is available. Do not promote the nominal endpoint to a working source without a successful authorised response or clarified official documentation.
- **Potential use:** procurement discovery if a public implementation is verified later; presently use the website as a pointer only.

#### RAB-GSA-20260923-30 — GSA IT Collect
- **Status / priority:** HISTORICAL PUBLIC DATA; medium for retrospective technology-spending accountability.
- **Evidence:** https://open.gsa.gov/api/itcollect/ ; attached PDF pp. 1–2; live page checked 2026-09-23.
- **Access / contents:** public key route to historical government-wide information-technology portfolio data; documentation links to the full API specification.
- **Critical correction:** agencies are no longer required to report through IT Collect. Do not treat it as a dependable current investment or AI-spending feed. Test actual coverage/end dates before comparisons and do not infer that missing updates mean cancelled investments.

### Collection decisions arising from this review

1. Start with Get Opportunities Public API plus Contract Awards public/revealed data. Keep broad USAspending collection for cost-efficient screening; enrich only selected awards through SAM.
2. Sample available statements of work and research solicitations before building large-scale news matching. The public notice API supplies attachment links, not guaranteed full text. Keep proposed/solicited, awarded, modified and completed activity separate.
3. Add acquisition subawards only after parent awards are reliably identified. Use exclusions and entity data as targeted enrichment, not loose name-only accusations.
4. Cache code lists and snapshots; use documented extract routes and careful date partitions where quotas make row-by-row calls impractical. A personal SAM key is different from an API.data.gov key; SearchGov has another credential scheme.
5. Test geography novelty against earlier public records and earlier reporting. None of the reviewed endpoints itself establishes that a base is previously unannounced. Recipient address, work location and ultimate equipment destination are different facts.
6. For the historical GDELT experiment, retain disclosure lag and the actual version available at the time. Absence of a match can mean unavailable documents, inaccessible text, late disclosure or retrieval failure, rather than journalistic neglect.

## SYS-0057 — IATI Datastore

- **Publisher:** International Aid Transparency Initiative
